global_monetary_reference_global_var_fixe = {
	set_global_variable = {
		name = money_value_reference_currency
		value = money_value_reference_currency_flux
	}
	set_global_variable = {
		name = money_value_rapported_inflation_reference_currency
		value = money_value_rapported_inflation_reference_currency_flux
	}
	set_global_variable = {
		name = money_supply_reference_currency
		value = money_supply_reference_currency_flux
	}
	set_global_variable = {
		name = money_supply_stockpile_by_other_country_reference_currency
		value = money_supply_stockpile_by_other_country_reference_currency_flux
	}
}

currency_of_player = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_dinar_currency
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = currency_of_player_is_dinar
			value = 1
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_dinar_algerian_dinar_currency

is_treaty_port_disable_pm_market_liquidity = {
	every_scope_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_treaty_port = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = offshore_port } remove_modifier = offshore_port }
			add_modifier = offshore_port
		}
	}
}

	#dinar
	money_value_dinar_global_var = {
		set_global_variable = {
			name = money_value_dinar_global_var
			value = {
				value = 0
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						has_modifier = dinar_leading_currency_type
					}
					add = money_value_target_real_in_gold #money_value_target_real #money_value
				}
			}
		}

			name = money_value_eco_west_african_eco_global_var
			value = {
				value = 0
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						has_modifier = eco_west_african_eco_leading_currency_type
						#not = {
						#	has_modifier = extreme_weak_currency
						#}
					}
					add = money_value_target_real_in_gold #money_value_target_real #money_value
				}
			}
		}
	}

global_monetary_reference_gui = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = global_monetary_reference
		}
		global_monetary_reference_1 = yes
	}
}

	fluctuations_money_value_1_year = {
		change_variable = {
			name = money_value_fixe_var_1_1Y
			add = 1
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				var:money_value_fixe_var_1_1Y = 1
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_var_half_1_year
				value = money_value_rapported_inflation
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:money_value_fixe_var_1_1Y = 2
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_var_1_year
				value = money_value_rapported_inflation
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_dif_1
				value = 0
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = money_value_dif_1
				add = var:money_value_var_1_year
			}
			change_variable = { #test
				name = money_value_dif_1
				subtract = var:money_value_var_half_1_year
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = money_value_dif_1
				divide = var:money_value_var_half_1_year
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_fixe_var_1_1Y
				value = 0
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					money_value_dif_1_final < -0.5
					not = {
						#protection de la devise lorsque le joeur ou y a change de standart monetaire de silver vers au-dessus, en effet la money value peut baisser alors que la parité à l'or reste la meme
						#2 manière d'avoir ce modificateur:
						#- soit changer le standart
						#- soit changer la valeur du standart -> n peut pas modifier aussi rapidement la valeur et donc ne déclancherai quoi qu'il en soit pas de crise
						has_modifier = foreign_exchange_controls
					}
				}
				#plus de protection comme pour central_bank_bankruptcy & country_bankruptcy
				currency_crisis = yes
			}
		}
	}
	fluctuations_money_value_5_year = {
		change_variable = {
			name = money_value_fixe_var_1_5Y
			add = 1
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				var:money_value_fixe_var_1_5Y = 1
			}
			set_variable = {

			}

			}

				value = 0
			}

			}
			change_variable = {
				name = money_value_dif_5

			}

	#monetary_policy_inflation
	monetary_policy_inflation = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				not = {
					has_variable = monetary_policy_inflation
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = monetary_policy_inflation
				value = 0
			}
		}

		#law_revaluation
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_revaluation
				has_modifier = revaluation_currency_25
				money_supply_state_ratio > 0.10
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = monetary_policy_inflation
				subtract = 0.25
			}
		}
		#law_devaluation
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_devaluation
				has_modifier = devaluation_currency_25
				money_supply_state_ratio > 0.10
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = monetary_policy_inflation
				add = 0.25
			}
		}

		################################################################################################

		if = {
			limit = {
				var:devaluation_on = 1
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = monetary_policy_inflation
				add = var:inflation_value_predicted_devaluation_month_valided
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:revaluation_on = 1
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = monetary_policy_inflation
				add = var:inflation_value_predicted_revaluation_month_valided
			}
		}
	}
	monetary_policy_inflation_reset = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:monetary_policy_inflation < -1
				var:devaluation_on = 0
				var:revaluation_on = 0
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = monetary_policy_inflation
				add = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:monetary_policy_inflation > 1
				var:devaluation_on = 0
				var:revaluation_on = 0
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = monetary_policy_inflation
				subtract = 1
			}
		}
	}
	monetary_policy_inflation_reset_law = {
		activate_law = law_type:law_no_monetary_policy
		random_scope_building = {
			limit = {
				is_building_group = bg_bank
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = devaluation_currency_25 } remove_modifier = devaluation_currency_25 }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = devaluation_currency_50 } remove_modifier = devaluation_currency_50 }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = devaluation_currency_75 } remove_modifier = devaluation_currency_75 }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = devaluation_currency_100 } remove_modifier = devaluation_currency_100 }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = revaluation_currency_25 } remove_modifier = revaluation_currency_25 }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = revaluation_currency_50 } remove_modifier = revaluation_currency_50 }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = revaluation_currency_75 } remove_modifier = revaluation_currency_75 }
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = revaluation_currency_100 } remove_modifier = revaluation_currency_100 }
		}
	}
	monetary_policy_inflation_base_rate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = rise_base_rate
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = monetary_policy_inflation
				add = -0.5
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = down_base_rate
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = monetary_policy_inflation
				add = 0.5
			}
		}
	}

	currency_strength_modifier = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = reference_currency
			}
			remove_modifier = reference_currency
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = reference_currency_no
			}
			remove_modifier = reference_currency_no
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = strong_currency
			}
			remove_modifier = strong_currency
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = balanced_currency
			}
			remove_modifier = balanced_currency
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = weak_currency
			}
			remove_modifier = weak_currency
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = extreme_weak_currency
			}
			remove_modifier = extreme_weak_currency
		}

		#################################################################
		#reference_currency
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_reference_currency = yes
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = reference_currency
				#multiplier = difference_with_average_gold_exchange_rate_currencies_revers_100
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				is_reference_currency_no = yes
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = reference_currency_no
			}
		}
		#################################################################
		#strong_currency
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_strong_currency = yes
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = strong_currency
				multiplier = difference_with_average_gold_exchange_rate_currencies_revers_100
			}
		}
		#################################################################
		#balanced_currency
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_balanced_currency = yes
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = balanced_currency
			}
		}
		#################################################################
		#weak_currency
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_weak_currency = yes
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = weak_currency
				multiplier = difference_with_average_gold_exchange_rate_currencies_revers_100
			}
		}
		#################################################################
		#extreme_weak_currency
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_extreme_weak_currency = yes
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = extreme_weak_currency
				multiplier = difference_with_average_gold_exchange_rate_currencies_revers_100
			}
		}
		#################################################################

		#################################################################

		#central_bank_bankruptcy
		if = {
			limit  = {
				#Valeur de la monaie <0.01 = definition crash BC
				has_modifier = extreme_weak_currency #çà veux dire çà money_value_in_gold < 0.01
				#on exclu les pays qui sont deja entré dans la bankruptcy :
				has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES = yes #un  pays  en  fiat  standart  à cause  d'une première  crise ne refera pas de crise en boucle, il sera bloqué en fiat  standart
				not  = {
					var:central_bank_bankruptcy = 1 #trigger uniquement pour pays pas deja dans le bankruptcy
					var:extreme_weak_currency_solution_count > 1 #idem sachant que ce code est directement ap ce qui va attribué le modifier extreme_weak_currency -> pas le temps encore de compter
				}
			}
			central_bank_bankruptcy = yes
		}

		#country_bankruptcy
		if = {
			limit  = {
				has_modifier = declared_bankruptcy
				not  = {
					#has_modifier = economic_instability
					var:country_bankruptcy = 1 #cette variable ne peut etre reset que si le moddifier declared_bankruptcy n'esxiste plus pour le pays donc qu'il ce soit fait reset par le country_ratting
				}
			}
			country_bankruptcy = yes
		}

		if = {
			limit  = {
				var:central_bank_bankruptcy = 1
				has_technology_researched = metalique_standard #ne peut appliquer le la  crise si en fiat avant metalique standart
				not = {
					money_supply_stockpile_by_other_country = 0
				}
			}
			root = {
				save_scope_as = target_country
			}

			if = {
				limit  = {
					has_modifier = has_central_bank
				}
				every_country = {
					if = {
						limit  = {
							has_modifier = has_central_bank
							not = {
								this = root
							}
						}
						save_scope_as = seller
						all_currency_resold = yes
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					or = {
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar_algerian_dinar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar_iraqi_dinar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar_libyan_dinar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar_moroccan_dirham > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar_omanian_rial > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar_qiran > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar_saudi_riyal > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar_serbian_dinar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar_tunisian_dinar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar_yugoslav_dinar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dollar_australian_dollar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dollar_canadian_dollar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dollar_caribbean_dollar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dollar_confederate_states_dollar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dollar_liberian_dollar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dollar_new_zealand_dollar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_dollar_united_states_dollar > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_eco_ariary > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_eco_central_african_eco > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_eco_east_african_eco > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_eco_ethiopian_birr > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_eco_ghanaian_pound > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_eco_nigerian_naira > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_eco_south_african_rand > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_eco_tuareg_ouguiya > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_eco_west_african_eco > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_franc_belgian_franc > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_franc_french_franc > 0
						var:debt_in_national_currency_franc_luxembourgish_franc > 0

	inflation_modifier = {
		# remove_modifier = deflation_country
		# remove_modifier = inflation_country

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = inflation_country
			}
			remove_modifier = inflation_country
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = deflation_country
			}
			remove_modifier = deflation_country
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				inflation_value > 0.02
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = inflation_country
				multiplier = inflation_value_for_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				inflation_value < -0.02
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = deflation_country
				multiplier = inflation_value_for_modifier
			}
		}

	fluctuations_silver_to_gold_rate_1_year = {
		change_variable = {
			name = silver_to_gold_rate_fixe_var_1_1Y
			add = 1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:silver_to_gold_rate_fixe_var_1_1Y = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:silver_to_gold_rate_fixe_var_1_1Y = 2
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = silver_to_gold_rate_fixe_var_1_1Y
				value = 0
			}
		}
	}

	money_value_target_modification = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				not = {
					or = {
						has_modifier = foreign_exchange_controls
						has_modifier = monetary_systeme_transition
					}
					AND = {
						#bloquer la revaluation des silver standart à partir de 1873 pour eviter des chose abrante sur le money value target
						game_date >= 1873.1.1
						has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard
					}
					#pour le bimetalisme ce callera sur l'or meme si les reserve sont majoritairement silver
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					devaluation_money_value_target_trigger = yes
					not = {
						or = {
							is_subject_custom_trigger = yes
							market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = yes
						}
					}
				}
				devaluation_money_value_target = yes

				#event player
				capital= {
					save_scope_as = capital_state
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
						scope:target_country = {
							scripted_money_value_target = no #test empeche UK et Mex d'avoir une notification pour indiquer une modification de parité inverse
						}
 					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.31
				}

				if = { limit = { has_modifier = devaluation_currency_target } remove_modifier = devaluation_currency_target }
				add_modifier = {
					name = devaluation_currency_target
					months = 12
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					revaluation_money_value_target_trigger = yes
					not = {
						or = {
							is_subject_custom_trigger = yes
							market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = yes
						}
					}
				}
				revaluation_money_value_target = yes

				#event player
				capital= {
					save_scope_as = capital_state
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
						scope:target_country = {
							scripted_money_value_target = no #test empeche UK et Mex d'avoir une notification pour indiquer une modification de parité inverse
						}
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.32
				}

				if = { limit = { has_modifier = revaluation_currency_target } remove_modifier = revaluation_currency_target }
				add_modifier = {
					name = revaluation_currency_target
					months = 12
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					or = {
						is_subject_custom_trigger = yes
						market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = yes
					}
				}
				subject_money_value_target = yes
			}
		}
	}
	devaluation_money_value_target = {
		#Scripted
		if = {
			limit = {
				scripted_money_value_target = yes
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1_old
				value = devaluation_money_value_target
			}
			devaluation_revaluation_money_value_target_scripted_silver_bimetallism = yes
			devaluation_revaluation_money_value_target_scripted_gold = yes
		}

		#Generic
		if = {
			limit = {
				not = {
					scripted_money_value_target = yes
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1_old
				value = var:money_value_target_1
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = devaluation_money_value_target
			}
		}
	}
	revaluation_money_value_target = {
		#Scripted
		if = {
			limit = {
				scripted_money_value_target = yes
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1_old
				value = var:money_value_target_1
			}
			devaluation_revaluation_money_value_target_scripted_silver_bimetallism = yes
			devaluation_revaluation_money_value_target_scripted_gold = yes
		}

		#Generic
		if = {
			limit = {
				not = {
					scripted_money_value_target = yes
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1_old
				value = var:money_value_target_1
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = revaluation_money_value_target
			}
		}
	}
	subject_money_value_target = {
		set_variable = {
			name = money_value_target_1
			value = overlord_money_value_target
		}
	}

	devaluation_revaluation_money_value_target_scripted_silver_bimetallism = {

		#On test les valeur historique, si elle vont dans le sens du jeux alors cela ajoute du flavor

		# #union latine silver
		# if = {
		# 	limit = {
		# 		or = {
		# 			has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard
		# 			has_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard
		# 		}
		# 		union_latine = yes
		# 	}
		# 	#question je set  le silver standard en fonction de la valeur à l'or
		# 	#equivalent en or
		# 	#0.29032 or
		# 	#4.50000 argent

	monetary_systeme_transition = {
		#Silver -> Bimetalisme
		if = {
			limit = {
				not = {
					has_modifier = monetary_systeme_transition
				}
				is_monetary_systeme_transition_valid_country_to_bimetalisme = yes
				is_monetary_systeme_transition_valid_country_to_gold_exchange_standard = no
			}
			#money_value_target_pre_set = yes
			activate_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard
			# central_bank_production_methods = yes
			# remove_modifier = foreign_exchange_controls
			# add_modifier = {
			# 	name = foreign_exchange_controls
			# 	months = 12
			# }
			# reset_debt_in_currency_for_monetary_systeme_transition = yes
			# set_variable = {
			# 	name = money_value_ta rget_1
			# 	value = money_value_target_bimetlic
			# }
			# trade_balance = yes
			# add_modifier = {
			# 	name = monetary_systeme_transition
			# 	months = 120
			# }
		}

		#Silver -> gold/Bimealisme -> Gold
		if = {
			limit = {
				not = {
					has_modifier = monetary_systeme_transition
				}
				is_monetary_systeme_transition_valid_country_to_gold = yes
				is_monetary_systeme_transition_valid_country_to_gold_exchange_standard = no
			}
			#money_value_target_pre_set = yes
			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard
			# central_bank_production_methods = yes
			# remove_modifier = foreign_exchange_controls
			# add_modifier = {
			# 	name = foreign_exchange_controls
			# 	months = 12
			# }
			# reset_debt_in_currency_for_monetary_systeme_transition = yes
			# set_variable = {
			# 	name = money_value_target_1
			# 	value = global_var:money_value_median
			# }
			# trade_balance = yes
			# add_modifier = {
			# 	name = monetary_systeme_transition
			# 	months = 120
			# }
		}

		#Gold -> gold_exchange_standard
		if = {
			limit = {
				not = {
					has_modifier = monetary_systeme_transition
				}
				is_monetary_systeme_transition_valid_country_to_gold_exchange_standard = yes
			}
			money_value_target_pre_set = yes
			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_exchange_standard
			# central_bank_production_methods = yes
			# central_bank_production_methods_3 = yes
			# remove_modifier = foreign_exchange_controls
			# add_modifier = {
			# 	name = foreign_exchange_controls
			# 	months = 12
			# }
			# #money_value_target_modification = yes
			# # set_variable = {
			# # 	name = money_value_target_1
			# # 	value = money_value
			# # }
			# set_variable = {
			# 	name = money_value_target_1
			# 	value = money_value_target_pre_set
			# }
			# trade_balance = yes
			# add_modifier = {
			# 	name = monetary_systeme_transition
			# 	months = 120
			# }
		}
	}

	###########################

	extreme_weak_currency_solution_player = {
		change_variable = {
			name = extreme_weak_currency_solution_count_player
			add = 1
		}
	}

	extreme_weak_currency_solution = {
		change_variable = {
			name = extreme_weak_currency_solution_count
			add = 1
		}
	}



	reset_debt_in_currency = {
		set_variable = {
			name = debt_in_national_currency_dinar
			value = 0

	devaluation_on = {
		change_variable = {
			name = devaluation_on_time
			add = 1
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				var:devaluation_on_time = devaluation_time_month
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = devaluation_on
				value = 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = var:money_value_target_valided
			}

			############# Reset
			set_variable = {
				name = devaluation_on_time
				value = 0
			}
			activate_law = law_type:law_no_monetary_policy #test
			monetary_policy_ideology_dynamic_reset = yes
		}
	}
	revaluation_on = {
		change_variable = {
			name = revaluation_on_time
			add = 1
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				var:revaluation_on_time = revaluation_time_month
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = revaluation_on
				value = 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = var:money_value_target_valided
			}

			############# Reset
			set_variable = {
				name = revaluation_on_time
				value = 0
			}
			activate_law = law_type:law_no_monetary_policy #test
			monetary_policy_ideology_dynamic_reset = yes
		}
	}

	monetary_policy_ideology_dynamic = {
	capital = {
		save_scope_as = capital_state
	}
	save_scope_as = target_country

	random_interest_group = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_monetary_policy }
		}
		save_scope_as = ig_want_a_large_monetary_policy
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:ig_want_a_large_monetary_policy
			OR = {
				central_bank_metal_reserves_state_to_ensure_the_conversion_pourcent > 1.25
				central_bank_metal_reserves_state_to_ensure_the_conversion_pourcent < 0.75
				zz_ef_rate_policy_steps >= 2
			}
		}
		trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.106
	}

	random_interest_group = {
		limit = {
			has_ideology = ideology:ideology_monetary_policy
		}
		save_scope_as = ig_no_want_a_large_monetary_policy
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:ig_no_want_a_large_monetary_policy
			central_bank_metal_reserves_state_to_ensure_the_conversion_pourcent < 1.25
			central_bank_metal_reserves_state_to_ensure_the_conversion_pourcent > 0.75
			zz_ef_rate_policy_steps < 2
		}
		trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.107
	}
}
	monetary_policy_ideology_dynamic_reset = {
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_monetary_policy
			}
			remove_ideology = ideology_monetary_policy
		}
	}
#brace

money_value_target_pre_set = {
	# Si standard argent
	if = {
		limit = {
			#has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard
			var:law_silver_standard ?= 1
		}
		#bimetalique
		set_variable = {
			name = money_value_target_1_bimetallic
			value = money_value_bimetallism_standard
		}
		#gold
		set_variable = {
			name = money_value_target_1_gold
			value = money_value_gold_standard
		}
	}
	# Si standard bimétallique
	if = {
		limit = {
			#has_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard
			var:law_bimetallism_standard ?= 1
		}
		#silver
		set_variable = {
			name = money_value_target_1_silver
			value = money_value_silver_standard
		}
		#gold
		set_variable = {
			name = money_value_target_1_gold
			value = money_value_gold_standard
		}
	}
	# Si standard or
	if = {
		limit = {
			#has_law = law_type:law_gold_standard
			var:law_gold_standard ?= 1
		}
		#silver
		set_variable = {
			name = money_value_target_1_silver
			value = money_value_silver_standard
		}
		#bimetalique
		set_variable = {
			name = money_value_target_1_bimetallic
			value = money_value_bimetallism_standard
		}
	}
}

set_reset_monetary_system_status = {

	set_variable = {
		name = law_no_monetary_system
		value = 0
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = law_fiat_standard
		value = 0
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = law_silver_standard
		value = 0
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = law_bimetallism_standard
		value = 0
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = law_gold_standard
		value = 0
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = law_gold_exchange_standard
		value = 0
	}

	###########################################################################################################################################################################################
	###########################################################################################################################################################################################
	###########################################################################################################################################################################################
	###########################################################################################################################################################################################

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_no_monetary_system
			has_law = law_type:law_bimetallic_ratio_no
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = law_no_monetary_system
			value = 1
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
			has_law = law_type:law_bimetallic_ratio_no
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = law_fiat_standard
			value = 1
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard
			has_law = law_type:law_bimetallic_ratio_no
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = law_silver_standard
			value = 1
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard
			has_law = law_type:law_bimetallic_ratio_15
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = law_bimetallism_standard
			value = 1
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_gold_standard
			has_law = law_type:law_bimetallic_ratio_no
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = law_gold_standard
			value = 1
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_gold_exchange_standard
			has_law = law_type:law_bimetallic_ratio_no
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = law_gold_exchange_standard
			value = 1
		}
	}
}
on_activate_monetary_system_law = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = extreme_weak_currency_solution_count }
				var:extreme_weak_currency_solution_count = 0
			}
		}
		zz_ef_std_switch_before = yes
		money_value_target_pre_set = yes
		central_bank_production_methods = yes
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = foreign_exchange_controls } remove_modifier = foreign_exchange_controls }
		add_modifier = {
			name = foreign_exchange_controls
			months = 12
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = money_value_target_1
			value = money_value_target_pre_set
		}
		zz_ef_std_switch_after = yes
		trade_balance = yes
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = monetary_systeme_transition } remove_modifier = monetary_systeme_transition }
		add_modifier = {
			name = monetary_systeme_transition
			months = 12
		}
		set_reset_monetary_system_status = yes
	}
}

on_activate_law_no_monetary_policy = {
	zz_ef_mp_clear = yes
}

on_activate_law_revaluation = {
	if = { limit = { has_modifier = devaluation_currency_25 } remove_modifier = devaluation_currency_25 }
	if = { limit = { has_modifier = revaluation_currency_25 } remove_modifier = revaluation_currency_25 }
	add_modifier = {
		name = revaluation_currency_25
	}
	zz_ef_mp_init = yes
}
on_activate_law_devaluation = {
	if = { limit = { has_modifier = revaluation_currency_25 } remove_modifier = revaluation_currency_25 }
	if = { limit = { has_modifier = devaluation_currency_25 } remove_modifier = devaluation_currency_25 }
	add_modifier = {
		name = devaluation_currency_25
	}
	zz_ef_mp_init = yes
}

stockpiling_currency = {
	if  = { 
		limit = { 	
			money_value_dinar > 0  
		} 
		stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { 
			currency = dinar 
		}  
	}
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_dinar_algerian_dinar > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = dinar_algerian_dinar }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_dinar_iraqi_dinar > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = dinar_iraqi_dinar }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_dinar_libyan_dinar > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = dinar_libyan_dinar }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_dinar_moroccan_dirham > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = dinar_moroccan_dirham }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_dinar_omanian_rial > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = dinar_omanian_rial }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_dinar_qiran > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = dinar_qiran }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_dinar_saudi_riyal > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = dinar_saudi_riyal }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_dinar_serbian_dinar > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = dinar_serbian_dinar }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_dinar_tunisian_dinar > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = dinar_tunisian_dinar }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_dinar_yugoslav_dinar > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = dinar_yugoslav_dinar }  }

	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_spe_drachma > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = spe_drachma }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_spe_korean_won > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = spe_korean_won }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_spe_latvian_lats > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = spe_latvian_lats }  }

	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_spe_peseta > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = spe_peseta }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_spe_ruble > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = spe_ruble }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_spe_uni > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = spe_uni }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_spe_yen > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = spe_yen }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_spe_yuan > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = spe_yuan }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_spe_zloti > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = spe_zloti }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_thaler_hannoveraner_thaler > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = thaler_hannoveraner_thaler }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_thaler_prussian_thaler > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = thaler_prussian_thaler }  }
	if  = { limit = { 	money_value_thaler_saxon_thaler > 0  } stockpiling_currency_type_1 = { currency = thaler_saxon_thaler }  }
}
stockpiling_currency_type_1 = {
	#il ne faut pas que des sujet no market owner puisse déclanché car le $currency$_c_market_goods_delta et donc le stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_2 sont positif quelque soit le market owner si il y a le  bien dans le marché

	#######################################################################
	#scope
	every_scope_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site
		}
	}

	#cela est pour récupérer la valeur de l'etat
	set_variable = {
		name = stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_1
		value = stockpiling_$currency$_state #récupèrer stockpiling_$currency$_state_1 soit la valeur de l'état
	}
	change_variable = {
		name = stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_1
		subtract = var:stockpiling_$currency$_c_in_reserve_currency #si un nouveau pays ce crer alors cette valeur sera de 2 est la totalité des reserve de l'etat sera equivalenbte à l'ancienne valeur dans l'etat et la valeur issiu de la reserve
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_2
		value = 0
	}

	#######################################################################
	#modification de la valeur dans la reserve de la bank centrale soit par politique soit par mainting simple

	#For country witch $currency$ law
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_$currency$_currency
		}

		#stockpiling in central bank currency reserve
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_1 > 0
				or = {
					not = {
						$currency$_c_market_goods_delta = 0
					}
					var:devaluation_on = 1
					var:revaluation_on = 1
				}
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_2
				add = $currency$_c_market_goods_delta
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_2
				multiply = 4
			}

			#politique monétaire devaluation
			if = { limit = { var:devaluation_on = 1 } change_variable = { name = stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_2 add = money_supply_to_add_per_month } }
			#politique monétaire réévaluation
			if = { limit = { var:revaluation_on = 1 } change_variable = { name = stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_2 add = money_supply_to_subtract_per_month } }

			change_variable = {
				name = stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_1
				add = var:stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_2
			}
		}

		#circulating_$currency$_c_1 #les pop
		set_variable = {
			name = circulating_$currency$_c_var_2
			value = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				$currency$_c_market_goods_buy_orders > 0
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_$currency$_c_var_2
				add = $currency$_c_market_goods_buy_orders
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_$currency$_c_var_2
				multiply = 4
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_$currency$_c_var_1
				add = var:circulating_$currency$_c_var_2
			}
		}
		#reset de la valeur de la reserve de la bc si négative
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_1 < 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_1
				value = 0
			}
		}
		#stockpiling_$currency$_c_state_1 -> remettre la valeur dans l'état
		if = {
			limit = {
				market_owner_is_root = yes
				var:stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_1 >= 0
 			}
			scope:central_bank_site = {
				if = {
					set_variable = {
						name = stockpiling_$currency$_state_1
						value = root.var:stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	#######################################################################

	#######################################################################
	#Trade balance sockpile and Stockpiling if $currency$ in market
	if = {
		limit = {
			market_owner_is_root = yes
			not = {
				has_law = law_type:law_$currency$_currency
			}
		}

		# #Stockpiling if $currency$ in market but no my currency -> plus de reserve de d'autre currency dans le marcher
		# if = {
		# 	limit = {
		# 		$currency$_c_market_goods_delta > 0
		# 	}
		# 	change_variable = {
		# 		name = stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_2
		# 		add = $currency$_c_market_goods_delta
		# 	}
		# 	change_variable = {
		# 		name = stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_2
		# 		multiply = 4
		# 	}
		# 	change_variable = {
		# 		name = stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_1
		# 		add = var:stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_2
		# 	}
		# 	scope:central_bank_site = {
		# 		if = {
		# 			set_variable = {
		# 				name = stockpiling_$currency$_state_1
		# 				value = root.var:stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_1
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# }

		#reserve_currency_$currency$
		if = {
			limit = {
				or  = {
					#export_in_$currency$ > 0
					export_to_$currency$ > 0 #revient au meme et beaucoup moinn de chiffre à géré et à calculé
					not  = {
						scope:central_bank_site.var:stockpiling_$currency$_reserve_currency_state_1 = owner.var:stockpiling_$currency$_c_in_reserve_currency
					}
				}
			}
			#quantité de devise stocké par la balanace du commerce
			change_variable = {
				name = stockpiling_$currency$_c_in_reserve_currency
				add = export_in_$currency$
			}
			scope:central_bank_site = {
				if = {
					set_variable = {
						name = stockpiling_$currency$_reserve_currency_state_1
						value = root.var:stockpiling_$currency$_c_in_reserve_currency
					}
				}
			}

			#ajout au gui du flux de l'accumulation
			change_variable = {
				name = stockpiling_$currency$_c_var_2
				add  = {
					add = export_in_$currency$
					divide = 4
				}
			}
		}
	}
	#debt_in_$currency$
	if = {
		limit = {
			import_in_$currency$ > 0
			import_export_disable = 0 #désactive l'accumulation de dette si le pays entre en crise
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = debt_in_national_currency_$currency$
			add = import_in_$currency$
		}
	}
	#######################################################################

	#all_$currency$_stored -> stock la valeur du stockpile + la valeur du commerce
	scope:central_bank_site = {
		set_variable = {
			name = all_$currency$_stored
			value = owner.stockpiling_$currency$_state  #var:stockpiling_$currency$_state_1
		}
	}
}

#brace
    #dinar
	sell_dinar_currency_crisis = {
	scope:seller = {
		save_scope_as = seller_root
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site_root
		}
	}
	scope:target_country = {
		save_scope_as = overrated_currency_country_owner
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_root
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
			scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dinar_currency
				sell_currency_crisis_1 = yes
			}
		}
		scope:seller_root = {
			# all of it, at the parity, as far as the issuer's metal goes
			set_variable = { name = stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var value = stockpiling_dinar_state }
			set_variable = {
				name = zz_ef_crisis_due
				value = {
					value = var:stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var
					multiply = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner.money_value_target_real
					min = 0
				}
			}
			zz_ef_crisis_redeem = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = stockpiling_dinar_c_in_reserve_currency
					var:stockpiling_dinar_c_in_reserve_currency > 0
				}
				set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dinar_c_in_reserve_currency value = 0 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site_root = {
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_dinar_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dinar_state_1 value = 0 } }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_dinar_reserve_currency_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dinar_reserve_currency_state_1 value = 0 } }
			}
		}
		scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
			reset_debt_in_national_currency = yes
		}
	}
}

			set_variable = {
				name = zz_ef_crisis_due
				value = {
					value = var:stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var
					multiply = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner.money_value_target_real
					min = 0
				}
			}
			zz_ef_crisis_redeem = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = stockpiling_dinar_serbian_dinar_c_in_reserve_currency
					var:stockpiling_dinar_serbian_dinar_c_in_reserve_currency > 0
				}
				set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dinar_serbian_dinar_c_in_reserve_currency value = 0 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site_root = {
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_dinar_serbian_dinar_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dinar_serbian_dinar_state_1 value = 0 } }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_dinar_serbian_dinar_reserve_currency_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dinar_serbian_dinar_reserve_currency_state_1 value = 0 } }
			}
		}
		scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
			reset_debt_in_national_currency = yes
		}
	}
}

				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place

				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes

			exists = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner

			exists = scope:central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
			scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dinar_tunisian_dinar_currency
				sell_currency_crisis_1 = yes
			}
		}

					multiply = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner.money_value_target_real
					min = 0
				}
			}
			zz_ef_crisis_redeem = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = stockpiling_dinar_tunisian_dinar_c_in_reserve_currency
					var:stockpiling_dinar_tunisian_dinar_c_in_reserve_currency > 0

		}
		scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
			reset_debt_in_national_currency = yes
		}
	}
}
    #dollar_united_states_dollar
	sell_dollar_united_states_dollar_currency_crisis = {
	scope:seller = {
		save_scope_as = seller_root
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site_root
		}
	}
	scope:target_country = {
		save_scope_as = overrated_currency_country_owner
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_root
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
			scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dollar_united_states_dollar_currency
				sell_currency_crisis_1 = yes
			}
		}
		scope:seller_root = {
			# all of it, at the parity, as far as the issuer's metal goes
			set_variable = { name = stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var value = stockpiling_dollar_united_states_dollar_state }
			set_variable = {
				name = zz_ef_crisis_due
				value = {
					value = var:stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var
					multiply = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner.money_value_target_real
					min = 0
				}
			}
			zz_ef_crisis_redeem = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = stockpiling_dollar_united_states_dollar_c_in_reserve_currency
					var:stockpiling_dollar_united_states_dollar_c_in_reserve_currency > 0
				}
				set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dollar_united_states_dollar_c_in_reserve_currency value = 0 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site_root = {
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_dollar_united_states_dollar_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dollar_united_states_dollar_state_1 value = 0 } }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_dollar_united_states_dollar_reserve_currency_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dollar_united_states_dollar_reserve_currency_state_1 value = 0 } }
			}
		}
		scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
			reset_debt_in_national_currency = yes
		}
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_root
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
			scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
				has_law = law_type:law_eco_west_african_eco_currency
				sell_currency_crisis_1 = yes
			}
		}
		scope:seller_root = {
			# all of it, at the parity, as far as the issuer's metal goes
			set_variable = { name = stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var value = stockpiling_eco_west_african_eco_state }
			set_variable = {
				name = zz_ef_crisis_due
				value = {
					value = var:stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var
					multiply = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner.money_value_target_real
					min = 0
				}
			}
			zz_ef_crisis_redeem = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = stockpiling_eco_west_african_eco_c_in_reserve_currency
					var:stockpiling_eco_west_african_eco_c_in_reserve_currency > 0
				}
				set_variable = { name = stockpiling_eco_west_african_eco_c_in_reserve_currency value = 0 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site_root = {
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_eco_west_african_eco_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_eco_west_african_eco_state_1 value = 0 } }

				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_gulden_indies_guilder_reserve_currency_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_gulden_indies_guilder_reserve_currency_state_1 value = 0 } }

    #gulden_south_german_gulden
	sell_gulden_south_german_gulden_currency_crisis = {
	scope:seller = {
		save_scope_as = seller_root
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site_root
		}
	}
	scope:target_country = {
		save_scope_as = overrated_currency_country_owner
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_root
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
			scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
				has_law = law_type:law_gulden_south_german_gulden_currency
				sell_currency_crisis_1 = yes
			}
		}
		scope:seller_root = {
			# all of it, at the parity, as far as the issuer's metal goes
			set_variable = { name = stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var value = stockpiling_gulden_south_german_gulden_state }
			set_variable = {
				name = zz_ef_crisis_due
				value = {
					value = var:stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var
					multiply = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner.money_value_target_real
					min = 0
				}
			}
			zz_ef_crisis_redeem = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = stockpiling_gulden_south_german_gulden_c_in_reserve_currency
					var:stockpiling_gulden_south_german_gulden_c_in_reserve_currency > 0
				}
				set_variable = { name = stockpiling_gulden_south_german_gulden_c_in_reserve_currency value = 0 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site_root = {
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_gulden_south_german_gulden_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_gulden_south_german_gulden_state_1 value = 0 } }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_gulden_south_german_gulden_reserve_currency_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_gulden_south_german_gulden_reserve_currency_state_1 value = 0 } }
			}
		}
		scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {

	}
	scope:target_country = {
		save_scope_as = overrated_currency_country_owner
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_root
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
			scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
				has_law = law_type:law_leon_lev_currency
				sell_currency_crisis_1 = yes
			}
		}
		scope:seller_root = {
			# all of it, at the parity, as far as the issuer's metal goes
			set_variable = { name = stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var value = stockpiling_leon_lev_state }
			set_variable = {
				name = zz_ef_crisis_due
				value = {
					value = var:stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var
					multiply = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner.money_value_target_real
					min = 0
				}
			}
			zz_ef_crisis_redeem = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = stockpiling_leon_lev_c_in_reserve_currency
					var:stockpiling_leon_lev_c_in_reserve_currency > 0
				}
				set_variable = { name = stockpiling_leon_lev_c_in_reserve_currency value = 0 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site_root = {
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_leon_lev_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_leon_lev_state_1 value = 0 } }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_leon_lev_reserve_currency_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_leon_lev_reserve_currency_state_1 value = 0 } }
			}
		}
		scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
			reset_debt_in_national_currency = yes
		}
	}
}

			}
		}
		scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
			reset_debt_in_national_currency = yes
		}
	}
}
    #peso_argentine_peso
	sell_peso_argentine_peso_currency_crisis = {
	scope:seller = {
		save_scope_as = seller_root
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site_root
		}
	}
	scope:target_country = {
		save_scope_as = overrated_currency_country_owner
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_root
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
			scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
				has_law = law_type:law_peso_argentine_peso_currency
				sell_currency_crisis_1 = yes
			}
		}
		scope:seller_root = {
			# all of it, at the parity, as far as the issuer's metal goes
			set_variable = { name = stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var value = stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_state }
			set_variable = {
				name = zz_ef_crisis_due
				value = {
					value = var:stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var
					multiply = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner.money_value_target_real
					min = 0
				}
			}
			zz_ef_crisis_redeem = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_c_in_reserve_currency
					var:stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_c_in_reserve_currency > 0
				}
				set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_c_in_reserve_currency value = 0 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site_root = {
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_state_1 value = 0 } }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_reserve_currency_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_reserve_currency_state_1 value = 0 } }
			}
		}
		scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
			reset_debt_in_national_currency = yes
		}
	}

		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_root
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
			scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
				has_law = law_type:law_peso_honduran_lempira_currency
				sell_currency_crisis_1 = yes
			}
		}
		scope:seller_root = {
			# all of it, at the parity, as far as the issuer's metal goes
			set_variable = { name = stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var value = stockpiling_peso_honduran_lempira_state }
			set_variable = {
				name = zz_ef_crisis_due
				value = {
					value = var:stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var
					multiply = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner.money_value_target_real
					min = 0
				}
			}
			zz_ef_crisis_redeem = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = stockpiling_peso_honduran_lempira_c_in_reserve_currency
					var:stockpiling_peso_honduran_lempira_c_in_reserve_currency > 0
				}
				set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_honduran_lempira_c_in_reserve_currency value = 0 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site_root = {
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_peso_honduran_lempira_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_honduran_lempira_state_1 value = 0 } }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_peso_honduran_lempira_reserve_currency_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_honduran_lempira_reserve_currency_state_1 value = 0 } }
			}
		}
		scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
			reset_debt_in_national_currency = yes
		}
	}
}

					multiply = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner.money_value_target_real
					min = 0
				}
			}
			zz_ef_crisis_redeem = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = stockpiling_pound_irish_pound_c_in_reserve_currency
					var:stockpiling_pound_irish_pound_c_in_reserve_currency > 0
				}
				set_variable = { name = stockpiling_pound_irish_pound_c_in_reserve_currency value = 0 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site_root = {
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_pound_irish_pound_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_pound_irish_pound_state_1 value = 0 } }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_pound_irish_pound_reserve_currency_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_pound_irish_pound_reserve_currency_state_1 value = 0 } }
			}
		}
		scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
			reset_debt_in_national_currency = yes
		}
	}
}

				sell_currency_crisis_1 = yes
			}
		}
		scope:seller_root = {
			# all of it, at the parity, as far as the issuer's metal goes
			set_variable = { name = stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var value = stockpiling_spe_latvian_lats_state }
			set_variable = {
				name = zz_ef_crisis_due
				value = {
					value = var:stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var
					multiply = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner.money_value_target_real
					min = 0
				}
			}
			zz_ef_crisis_redeem = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = stockpiling_spe_latvian_lats_c_in_reserve_currency
					var:stockpiling_spe_latvian_lats_c_in_reserve_currency > 0
				}
				set_variable = { name = stockpiling_spe_latvian_lats_c_in_reserve_currency value = 0 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site_root = {
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_spe_latvian_lats_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_spe_latvian_lats_state_1 value = 0 } }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_spe_latvian_lats_reserve_currency_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_spe_latvian_lats_reserve_currency_state_1 value = 0 } }
			}
		}
		scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
			reset_debt_in_national_currency = yes
		}
	}
}

				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site_root
		}
	}
	scope:target_country = {
		save_scope_as = overrated_currency_country_owner
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_root
			exists = scope:central_bank_site_overrated_currency_owner
			scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
				has_law = law_type:law_thaler_saxon_thaler_currency
				sell_currency_crisis_1 = yes
			}
		}
		scope:seller_root = {
			# all of it, at the parity, as far as the issuer's metal goes
			set_variable = { name = stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var value = stockpiling_thaler_saxon_thaler_state }
			set_variable = {
				name = zz_ef_crisis_due
				value = {
					value = var:stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var
					multiply = scope:overrated_currency_country_owner.money_value_target_real
					min = 0
				}
			}
			zz_ef_crisis_redeem = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = stockpiling_thaler_saxon_thaler_c_in_reserve_currency
					var:stockpiling_thaler_saxon_thaler_c_in_reserve_currency > 0
				}
				set_variable = { name = stockpiling_thaler_saxon_thaler_c_in_reserve_currency value = 0 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site_root = {
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_thaler_saxon_thaler_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_thaler_saxon_thaler_state_1 value = 0 } }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = stockpiling_thaler_saxon_thaler_reserve_currency_state_1 } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_thaler_saxon_thaler_reserve_currency_state_1 value = 0 } }
			}
		}
		scope:overrated_currency_country_owner = {
			reset_debt_in_national_currency = yes
		}
	}
}

reset_debt_in_national_currency_player = {
	#dinar
	if  = {
		limit  = {
			scope:player_country = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dinar_currency
			}
			var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar > 0
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = debt_in_national_currency_dinar
			subtract = scope:player_country.var:currency_quantity
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar < 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = debt_in_national_currency_dinar
				value = 0
			}
		}
	}

stockpiling_capital_state_transfert = {
	financial_center_modifier = yes

	every_scope_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}

		}
		if = {

				if = { limit = { has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place } remove_modifier = central_bank_historic_place }

				add_modifier = {
					name = looted_state
					months = 3
				}
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				var:financial_center_site_var = 0
				or = {
					var:stockpiling_bond_var_state_1 > 0
					var:stockpiling_manufacture_stock_var_state_1 > 0
					var:stockpiling_agricultural_stock_var_state_1 > 0
					var:stockpiling_mining_stock_var_state_1 > 0
					var:stockpiling_railroad_stock_var_state_1 > 0
				}
			}
		}
		stockpiling_capital_state_transfert_financial_center_place = yes
	}
}
stockpiling_capital_state_transfert_financial_center_place = {
	every_scope_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:financial_center_site_var =  1
			}
			save_scope_as = financial_center_site_capital #non nécessairement la capital
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:financial_center_site_var = 0
				not = {
					var:looted_state = 1
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = financial_center_site_no_capital #non nécessairement la capital
		}
	}
	if  = {
		limit  = {
			exists = scope:financial_center_site_no_capital
			#exists = scope:financial_center_site_capital
		}

		#actual Capital
		scope:financial_center_site_capital = {


			#####################
			#financial product
			#####################

			change_variable = {
				name = stockpiling_bond_var_state_1
				add = {
					scope:financial_center_site_no_capital = {
						add = var:stockpiling_bond_var_state_1
					}
				}
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = stockpiling_manufacture_stock_var_state_1
				add = {
					scope:financial_center_site_no_capital = {
						add = var:stockpiling_manufacture_stock_var_state_1
					}
				}
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = stockpiling_agricultural_stock_var_state_1
				add = {
					scope:financial_center_site_no_capital = {
						add = var:stockpiling_agricultural_stock_var_state_1
					}
				}
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = stockpiling_mining_stock_var_state_1
				add = {
					scope:financial_center_site_no_capital = {
						add = var:stockpiling_mining_stock_var_state_1
					}
				}
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = stockpiling_railroad_stock_var_state_1
				add = {
					scope:financial_center_site_no_capital = {
						add = var:stockpiling_railroad_stock_var_state_1
					}
				}
			}
		}

		#old capital
		scope:financial_center_site_no_capital = {
			if  = {
				limit  = {
					exists = scope:financial_center_site_no_capital
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = looted_state
					value = 1
				}


				#####################
				#financial product
				#####################

				set_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_bond_var_state_1
					value = 0
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_manufacture_stock_var_state_1
					value = 0
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_agricultural_stock_var_state_1
					value = 0
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_mining_stock_var_state_1
					value = 0
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_railroad_stock_var_state_1
					value = 0
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
enemy_capital_is_occuped = {
	set_variable  = {
		name = looting_1_year
		value = 1
	}

	every_scope_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site_capital
		}
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			is_at_war = yes
			has_war_with = ROOT
		}
		capital = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					controller = ROOT
					has_modifier = central_bank_place

				}
				save_scope_as = occupied_capital
			}
		}
	}

	if  = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:occupied_capital
		}

		scope:central_bank_site_capital = {

			owner  = {
				change_infamy = 50
			}

			#####################
			#gold
			#####################

			change_variable = {
				name = gold_state_1
				add = {
					scope:occupied_capital = {
						add = var:gold_state_1
						multiply = 0.5
					}
				}
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = silver_state_1
				add = {
					scope:occupied_capital = {
						add = var:silver_state_1

reset_debt_in_national_currency = {
	#dinar
	if  = {
		limit  = {
			scope:seller_root = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dinar_currency
			}
			var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar > 0
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = debt_in_national_currency_dinar
			subtract = scope:seller_root.var:stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar < 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = debt_in_national_currency_dinar
				value = 0
			}
		}
	}
	#dinar_algerian_dinar
	if  = {
		limit  = {
			scope:seller_root = {
				has_law = law_type:law_dinar_algerian_dinar_currency

		}

			subtract = scope:seller_root.var:stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:debt_in_national_currency_dinar_algerian_dinar < 0
			}

			var:debt_in_national_currency_spe_lithuanian_litas > 0
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = debt_in_national_currency_spe_lithuanian_litas
			subtract = scope:seller_root.var:stockpiling_overrated_quantity_var
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:debt_in_national_currency_spe_lithuanian_litas < 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = debt_in_national_currency_spe_lithuanian_litas
				value = 0
			}
		}
	}

remove_suject_currency = {
	activate_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard #law_no_monetary_system #Si on laisse law_no_monetary_system le systeme retourne jamais au silver standart il reste bloquer en no_monetary et utilise sa currency comme sourece de metal ou celle de l'overlord

	macro_facilities_on_action_bc = yes #On reprend completement

	# #a verifier

	# 	limit = {
	# 		or = {
	# 			var:law_no_monetary_system = 1
	# 			AND = {

	# 				var:v = 0
	# 				var:law_silver_standard = 0
	# 				var:law_bimetallism_standard = 0

	# 				var:law_gold_exchange_standard = 0
	# 			}
	# 		}

	# 	activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard #minimum à voir si autre chose ap 1873
	# }
	# if = {
	# 	limit = {
	# 		var:law_fiat_standard = 1
	# 	}
	# 	activate_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
	# }
	# if = {
	# 	limit = {
	# 		var:law_silver_standard = 1
	# 	}
	# 	activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard
	# }
	# if = {
	# 	limit = {
	# 		var:law_bimetallism_standard = 1
	# 	}
	# 	activate_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard
	# }
	# if = {
	# 	limit = {
	# 		var:law_gold_standard = 1
	# 	}
	# 	activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard
	# }
	# if = {

	# 		var:law_gold_exchange_standard = 1
	# 	}
	# 	activate_law = law_type:law_gold_exchange_standard
	# }

	money_value_target_modification = yes #il faut recalculer la target à verifier

	#pas de reset auto, si le pays ce crash à la libration il passe par la vois normal de la faillité

	#Event for player

	#empecher les pays revoluitionnaire d'envoyer des notif c'est chiant

	capital= {
		save_scope_as = capital_state
	}
	save_scope_as = target_country

	every_country = {
		limit = {
			is_player = yes
		}
		trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.29
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			not = {
				has_law = law_type:law_gold_exchange_standard
			}
		}
		central_bank_production_methods_4 = yes
	}
}
# В3.2 (5.10, runs s1b / s1c): E&F's monthly pulse (ef_on_monthly_pulse_recurence) puts a subject with a CB inside its
# overlord's market on law_external_exchange_standard (subject_currency) every month it is not on it; the currency
# zone (zz_ef_currency_zone.txt) then put it back on the overlord's standard -- a standard switch every month for
# Hanover, the Canadas, Finland, the Dutch East Indies... (each with E&F's 12 months of foreign_exchange_controls).
# A subject in the zone (var:zz_ef_cur_zone) is skipped here; any other -- E&F's body as is.
# !! MAINTENANCE !! Key-level REPLACE_OR_CREATE of E&F's subject_currency (01_economic_scripted_effects.txt):
# E&F's body as of 04.07.2026 inside the else.
subject_currency = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = zz_ef_cur_zone } }
		activate_law = law_type:law_external_exchange_standard
		money_value_target_modification = yes
		capital = {
			save_scope_as = capital_state
		}
		save_scope_as = target_country
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_player = yes
				game_date >= 1837.1.1
			}
			trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.28
		}
	}
}
